**🔴 RETRACTION — `#261` did not batch 21 jobs into 4.** The 8/25 entry read only the first bullet of `48d0b570`'s squashed body (*"batch 21 jobs into 4 — 58% of the Actions bill was the billing floor"*) and reported it as shipped fact. **The second bullet in that same message reverts it:** *"revert the job batching, bound every job's runtime instead — Reverts `925b4afd` on this branch."* Verified four ways, none of them the commit message: top-level job keys in `compliance-gate.yml` are **21 at `48d0b570^`, `48d0b570`, `ad196dc7` and `origin/main`**; the commit's diffstat on that file is **28 insertions / 0 deletions**; all three post-merge push runs (`32900120322`, `32913788576`, `32937536049`) ran **21 jobs each**; and the file today carries **21 `timeout-minutes`, 21 `actions/checkout`, 0 `if:`**. **Cause of the revert, and it is the more useful finding:** `scripts/audit-client-ip-probe-contract.mjs` freezes this workflow's shape — exactly 21 checkouts, 16 `setup-node`, 5 `setup-deno`, zero `if:`/`continue-on-error`, `paths:` containing `'**'` — as a **security invariant**. Consolidating meant editing that guard, i.e. an agent loosening a tamper-evidence control on the compliance gate, authored by the lane that stood to gain from it. The lane self-reverted and shipped the non-invasive half. **Downstream corrections:** expected saving is **~$5–8/month, not ~$27**, and the billing-floor arithmetic still describes current spend — run `32937536049` executed 21 jobs in **71 s of wall time (06:17:52 → 06:19:03)** and was billed 21 minutes. **What did ship and is worth keeping:** `timeout-minutes` on all 21 jobs (15, or 25 for the two Playwright jobs) — the direct fix for the 8/18 four-hour hang that billed ~720 minutes from a single run — plus a PR-scoped supersession group (`cancel-in-progress: ${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request' }}`). **Durable rule, third reading/counting correction in a week: a squashed PR body can contain a later commit reverting an earlier one. Read the whole body, and confirm structural claims against the merged file rather than the message.**

### 2026-08-11 (Tuesday Auto-Refresh — **FOUR-PR OVERNIGHT CHAIN ON THE PROTECTED LEAD PATH; THE OWNER-ALERT LANES COULD COLLAPSE ONTO ONE MAILBOX; `handle-lead` IS NOW DEPLOYED**) — Nine Commits Advance `origin/main` `ab39598` → `73ba3bf`, Four PRs Merged (`#224`–`#227`), 10 Files / +1,592 / −254

- **`#227` — `fix: keep Utah owner notification routes distinct` (`73ba3bf`, 09:18 UTC; commits `26351e4` + `b367588`).** A **controlled production lead** proved the primary and backup notification jobs could both resolve to the same mailbox when the live backup-recipient secret matched the primary recipient. Per the PR body the live secret was already corrected and independently verified, and the production impact audit found **no real customer leads** — only the controlled test row, since marked as test data. Fix: normalize and **require distinct** primary/backup routes; capture the durable lead + outbox row **before** routing checks; block duplicate and stale role-mismatched delivery before claim and before provider fetch; preserve valid-lane independence when only one recipient is malformed; **keyset-page the ready and expired queues so blocked rows cannot starve valid work** out of the 25-ready / 10-expired caps; scan active frozen routes in health-only mode, failing closed on mismatch, partial payload, query, cursor, or response-shape errors. **Verification:** 59 shared Deno tests, 21 owner-notification source-contract tests, 3 intake source tests, all 11 Edge entrypoints typechecked. Utah SMS secrets remain absent; no owner-response or text-monitor sends enabled; no second controlled lead submitted.

Adversarial fixtures + reviewed source pins + runbook guidance added. Independent security diff review: no blocking findings. Independent pin audit: **36/36**.
- **`#225` — `fix: accept exact gateway CF header rejection` (`086f519`/`6d2069f`, 07:24 UTC).** A read-only smoke against the Utah edge showed that a caller-supplied `cf-connecting-ip` returns an **exact HTTP 403 with body SHA-256 `dc0c6785aa92ab46bea7162d2b2e8702f60d93f35850cf8a2ea1ba6d842ac6c2`**, while baseline, `x-real-ip` and `x-forwarded-for` reach the function. The gateway rejection is now modeled exactly and signed under a distinct fail-closed schema for both native transport families; runtime and fingerprint binding retained for the six requests that reach the probe. The protected canary independently reproduced the forged-CF non-JSON rejection and failed closed before any protected deployment.
- **`#224` — `Fix Supabase probe body receipt binding` (`55eb…`/`19df388`, 06:54 UTC).** **Production incident:** a protected recovery attempt on 8/11 stopped **before deploying `handle-lead`** because the old gate wrongly required Supabase's provider-row `ezbr_sha256` and the repacked Management API body digest to be byte-identical. Exact-downloaded-source equality, dual-stack runtime binding, cleanup and signed-receipt checks all retained. The probe and its disposable host were removed and verified absent; **no lead, email or SMS send occurred.**

### 2026-08-10 midday (Monday FOURTH Auto-Refresh — **THE LARGEST SINGLE-PR HARDENING IN THIS LOG LANDS ON THE PROTECTED DEPLOY PATH, AND IT SHIPS WITH A FOUR-STEP OWNER CHECKLIST THAT IS NOT DONE**) — One Commit Advances `origin/main` `731b42b` → `25a6d58`, One PR Merged (#210), 14 Files / +7,039 / −717; PR Board Empty a Third Time, Corpus Unchanged, CI Green

- **Window:** `731b42b` → **`25a6d58`** — one commit, `Harden Utah client-IP deployment receipts (#210)` (Rconman99, merged **2026-08-10 16:03:28 UTC**, 14 files / **+7,039 / −717**). No content, no schema, no NAP/GBP surface, no sitemap change. All of it is CI, deploy-gating, and Edge-function probe tooling.
- **🟢 What `#210` actually does.** It replaces the client-IP deployment receipt with a **fail-closed v3 gate in front of the protected `handle-lead` / `lead-crm` deploy path** — closing the thread the 8/10 late-night refresh opened when `#205` (`f302ded`, 00:38) was built and then contained by `5f5c799` **49 minutes later**. Mechanics: (a) **offline Ed25519 issuance is separated from a public-key-only CI verifier**, so CI never holds a signing secret; (b) each receipt is **bound to one function, one exact `main` SHA, one project, and one dispatch nonce, with a 15-minute lifetime**; (c) duplicate-key/raw-secret artifacts and stale, reused, cross-function, or drifted receipts are rejected; (d) the complete **live function + secret metadata and probe absence are re-checked immediately before deploy, inside the same shell step** — closing the TOCTOU window between verification and action; (e) Production credentials and deploy capability are structurally restricted to the exact reviewed workflow; (f) opaque binary/archive execution and the reproduced workflow/parser bypass corpus are blocked; (g) signer-attested, non-atomic, and authorized-review trust boundaries are retained explicitly rather than assumed.

### 2026-08-07 late-night (Friday FOURTH Auto-Refresh — **THE GSC QUERY VIEW WAS ~97% BLIND; THE PAGE DIMENSION FIXES IT, AND THE MEASURED DATA REWRITES THE CONTENT STRATEGY**) — Seven Commits Advance `origin/main` `9a4ccfd` → `ce2ef5b`, Five PRs Merged (#194/#195/#196/#197/#198), Corpus Unchanged, CI 100% Green

- **#194 `01d65d6` — GSC page dimension + honest query coverage (7 files, +571/−25).** The stored query slice covered **2,107 of 85,434 impressions (2.5%)** and the readout never said so. Adds `gsc.top_pages` + a report-only "pages earning impressions but zero clicks" section (query rows are a bounded slice and per-query positions cannot be averaged into a page position); makes `top_queries` the **union of top 200 by clicks and top 200 by impressions** (the API sorts clicks-desc only, so zero-click queries sank below every 1-click query); raises fetch depth **1000 → 5000**; reports coverage as an **impression share** on the GSC totals line so it renders on a first snapshot; counts storage drops in `truncated`, not just the fetch `rowLimit`. The diff is **experiment-aware** — candidates under a running experiment are held back **and reported**, never silently dropped (no experiments exist in this market yet; the path is covered by a regression test). Two porting slips fixed: concurrency group read `seo-loop-texas`, and **CI never ran the loop's unit tests here**. Tests now gate PRs touching the scripts and gate the daily job, which is excluded on PRs so it can never push from one.

**3. 🟢 THE NATIVE SEO LOOP (#193 `f5b6853`) — largest infrastructure add since the cloud migration.** Method ported from the `seo-god` skill, **implemented natively: no Docker, no OpenSEO container, zero npm dependencies.** New files: `.github/workflows/seo-loop.yml` (79), `scripts/seo-crawl.mjs` (450 — real status codes, redirect chains, noindex, thin content), `scripts/seo-snapshot.mjs` (171), `scripts/seo-diff.mjs` (312 — regression-first ordering: regressions → quick wins at positions 4–15 → new queries), `scripts/seo-merge-ai-visibility.mjs` (72), `scripts/lib/gsc.mjs` (164), `scripts/seo-crawl.test.mjs` (66) + `scripts/seo-diff.test.mjs` (138), `docs/seo/SEO-LOOP.md` (63), plus `.vercelignore` entries. Three design properties worth preserving:
- **Report-only by contract** — the loop *never* edits pages. Content changes stay human decisions behind the compliance gate.
- **Fail-closed** — a failed crawl writes **no** snapshot and fails the job, on the stated reasoning that "a fabricated *0 issues* snapshot would read as a clean bill of health in tomorrow's diff." This is the 8/5 AEO silent-failure lesson applied preemptively.
- **Deadman** — the diff opens with a missed-run warning and a `::warning::` annotation when the previous snapshot is >48h old, so a silently-dead schedule surfaces in the next readout rather than never.

**4. 🟢 FIRST REAL GSC BASELINE ON RECORD (`7faffd3`, run `31221090726`).** `data/seo/snapshots/2026-08-07.json` (1,392 lines) + `data/seo/readouts/2026-08-07.md`.
- **Crawl: 151 pages, 148 indexable, 0 error issue types / 0 URLs.** Only `noindex-page` (warn) × 1 and `orphan-page` (info) × 2. Technically clean.
- **GSC: 73 clicks / 85,434 impressions, 2026-07-08 → 2026-08-04** (window ends ~3 days back). ≈**0.09% sitewide CTR** against strong impression volume — the impressions exist, the clicks do not.

- **Two `page_one_low_ctr` experiment candidates** (shaped for `frame-seo-experiment.v1`, human decision behind the compliance gate), both page-one rankings earning **zero clicks**:

- **AI visibility: not measured. Tracked keywords: none** — `data/seo/keywords.json` does not exist yet. Creating it is the obvious next step.

**Read on the two candidates:** title tag / meta description / SERP snippet work on those two location pages is the highest-ROI, fastest-payback SEO task currently identified anywhere in this repo. The rankings are already earned; only click-through is missing. Impact **HIGH**, time-to-results **days-to-weeks** (no new content, no new links, no crawl-and-index delay — just a re-crawl of an existing page-one URL).

### 2026-06-18 (Thursday EVENING — captured in the 6/25 Auto-Refresh) — Phase 0 Inbound Spam-Call Screening Lands on `main`: `handle-call` v2 → v3 + Two New Migrations (`dc4e63a`, 4 Files / +272 / −55) — Blocklist Hangup / Utah-Area-Code Pass-Through / Out-of-State "Press 1" Human-Gate (Robocalls Dead-End to Voicemail With No Lead), Plus Per-Number Commission Attribution (`leads.call_source` + `commission_eligible`); Both Blocklist and Human-Gate FAIL OPEN; Refresh Commit Built in an Isolated `git worktree` Off `origin/main` (Primary Tree Mid-Session on `feat/utah-crm-parity-texas-backport-2026-06-14`)

`dc4e63a` (Ryan-authored, 6/18 19:04 MDT) is the only commit on `origin/main` since the 6/18 midday refresh (`5328f5d`). `git show --stat` = 4 files, +272 / −55: `supabase/functions/handle-call/index.ts` (heavy rewrite, header comment rebranded "Frame Roofing Utah" → "Frame Restoration Utah"), `supabase/migrations/20260616_blocked_callers.sql` (NEW, +25), `supabase/migrations/20260617_lead_call_source.sql` (NEW, +11), `.vercelignore` (+1).

**The v3 router:** inbound calls now branch three ways — (a) a number in the new `blocked_callers` table gets a polite hangup (no Landon ring, no auto-created lead); (b) a Utah area code (801 / 385 / 435) rings straight through (frictionless — essentially all real customers); (c) anything else (out-of-state or no caller-ID, where spam skews) hits a `<Gather numDigits=1 timeout=8>` "press 1 to connect" gate — press 1 → create lead + dial Landon (proves a human), no input → voicemail with **no lead** (robo-dialers land here). Lead creation for screened calls is deferred to the `/connect` handler so spam never becomes a `general` lead. Both the blocklist read and the human-gate **fail open** — a DB error or a missing `blocked_callers` table degrades to ringing, never to dropping a real call. `LANDON_PHONE` forwarding is unchanged (`+14353024422`, internal-only).

**The two migrations + commission attribution:** `20260616_blocked_callers.sql` creates an E.164-primary-key blocklist (`phone` / `reason` / `source` / `created_at`), RLS-enabled with no policies (service-role-only, invisible to the anon key), seeded with `+18333380180` (the cold-pitch SEO solicitation auto-classified spam back on 2026-05-16). `20260617_lead_call_source.sql` adds nullable `leads.call_source` + `leads.commission_eligible` (additive / idempotent, no default, no constraint — the GENERATED margin-based `commission` column is untouched). A new `NUMBER_SOURCES` map in `handle-call` classifies each dialed tracking number: `+14352928802` (GBP + website NAP) → `google_website` / commission-eligible; directory tracking lines (HomeAdvisor / Yelp / Angi, added once provisioned) → non-commission; unknown dialed numbers default non-commission so the ledger never over-counts Ryan's leads. `isOurBusinessNumber()` lets a second tracking number through the previously single-number `creds.phone` webhook check.

### 2026-06-08 (Monday EVENING Auto-Refresh — POST-ACTIVITY, refresh #2) — Relay/Twilio Security + Reliability Hardening Burst: 6 Merged PRs (#82, #84, #85, #86, #87, #88) Land an Afternoon "Utah Parity Port" of the Hardened Twilio Relay Layer Into UT's Supabase Edge Functions — CRITICAL Spoofable-Webhook Fix (Twilio Signature Validation, new `_shared/twilio-verify.ts`), Webhook Idempotency (`processed_webhooks` Table + `claimWebhook()`), A2P Opt-Out + `.or()` Query-Injection Hardening on `handle-sms`/`handle-call`, and a New Hardened `outbound-call` Edge Function

Six PRs merged to `main` this afternoon (13:18–13:58 MDT, ~40-minute burst), advancing HEAD `30d94b2` (11:08 MDT morning auto-refresh) → `2f358df` (= `origin/main` = `main`). Note PR **#83 is skipped** in the sequence (closed/superseded, not merged). All six are a **"Utah parity port"**: the TX v2 systems layer (per `PORT-PLAN.md`) had a hardened Twilio relay UT never received, and this session backports that hardening into UT's call/SMS edge functions — *adapting*, not overwriting, since UT's `handle-call`/`handle-sms` were explicitly ahead on lead-auto-creation. `git diff --stat 30d94b2 HEAD` = **6 files, +448 / −48**.

**PR #82 (`014edca`, 13:18 MDT) — `fix(relay): backport Twilio signature validation (CRITICAL — spoofable webhooks)`:** new shared module `supabase/functions/_shared/twilio-verify.ts` (114 lines) that validates the `X-Twilio-Signature` HMAC on inbound webhook requests. Before this, `handle-call`/`handle-sms` accepted any POST to their public Function URLs — anyone could forge Twilio callbacks (spoof inbound calls/SMS → forged `call_logs` rows + auto-created junk leads, or trigger TwiML forwarding). This is the security root-fix of the burst.

**PR #84 (`ef4720b`, 13:40 MDT) — `fix(relay): Utah parity port pt.1 — sticky-TTL, A2P opt-out, .or() injection`:** hardens `handle-sms` — sticky-TTL (conversation routing window), **A2P opt-out** handling (STOP/UNSUBSCRIBE compliance — required for 10DLC carrier policy), and a fix for `.or()` Supabase query-string injection (unsanitized phone/body interpolated into `.or()` filters).

**PR #85 (`1191ea9`, 13:44 MDT) — `fix(relay): Utah parity pt.2 — webhook idempotency (processed_webhooks + claimWebhook)`:** new migration `supabase/migrations/20260608_relay_processed_webhooks.sql` (31 lines) adds a `processed_webhooks` table; new `claimWebhook()` guard makes webhook processing idempotent so Twilio's automatic retries (on 11200/timeout) can't double-insert calls/leads/SMS.

**PR #86 (`858b727`, 13:46 MDT) — `feat(relay): Utah parity pt.3 — add hardened outbound-call to the repo`:** adds `supabase/functions/outbound-call/index.ts` (106 lines) — a hardened outbound-call edge function (click-to-call / callback origination), now source-of-truth in the repo rather than deployed-only.

**PR #87 (`72722ff`, 13:57 MDT) — `fix(relay): Utah parity pt.4 — handle-call .or() injection, idempotency, CallSid`:** applies the same hardening to `handle-call` — `.or()` injection fix, idempotency (via the #85 `processed_webhooks` path), and proper `CallSid` handling/dedupe.

**PR #88 (`2f358df`, 13:58 MDT) — `chore(deploy): add outbound-call to the edge-function deploy allowlist`:** one-line add of `outbound-call` to `.github/workflows/deploy-edge-function.yml` so the new function is deployable via the manual edge-fn deploy workflow.

**Files touched (30d94b2→HEAD):** `supabase/functions/_shared/twilio-verify.ts` (NEW, +114) · `supabase/functions/handle-call/index.ts` (+130/−~ heavy) · `supabase/functions/handle-sms/index.ts` (+114) · `supabase/functions/outbound-call/index.ts` (NEW, +106) · `supabase/migrations/20260608_relay_processed_webhooks.sql` (NEW, +31) · `.github/workflows/deploy-edge-function.yml` (+1).

**Why it matters:** review velocity + response rate is the top local-pack ranking factor, and a review CTA silently pointing at a dead listing is a zero-feedback failure — every "leave a review" click was lost. The bug class is now fixed in prod, made un-mergeable (merge-time gate), and watched in production (weekly monitor). New identity facts captured for downstream tooling: **canonical GBP `cid 8458659884566588108`, `place_id ChIJCT6-aZD1TYcRzCpwX1kyY3U`, canonical GBP name "Frame Restoration Utah", 27 reviews, known-dead listing `cid 16833…`.**

### 2026-05-27 (Wednesday Evening Auto-Refresh — POST-ACTIVITY) — Quiet Streak Broken: Two Production Commits Flip a Major Architectural Decision in 53 Minutes — (1) `80a59d7` Root-Causes + Fixes the 15-Day `phone_clicks` DB Hole (CORS Preflight Drop on `application/json` Beacon → Switch to `text/plain` + `keepalive` fetch, Cache-Bust `?v=2` Across 124 Files, Backfill 13 Historical Rows from PostHog) + (2) `2fe52d2` Public NAP Swapped from 435-302-4422 → 435-292-8802 Across 164 Files (1,341 Insertions / 1,341 Deletions — Twilio Tracking Number IS Now the Public NAP; Closes the GBP-Call-Button Attribution Gap That Bypassed the Website Entirely) + 3 Location-Page Niche-Diff Edits Carry-Forward CLOSED (Swept Into `80a59d7` Cache-Bust Pass) + 2 New Untracked Artifacts: `data/GBP-SERVICES-PROPOSAL-2026-05-27.md` + `data/NAP-DIRECTORY-CHECKLIST-2026-05-27.md`
**Two commits landed today after a two-blank-day quiet streak**, both authored by `Review Bot <review-bot@frameroofingutah.com>` 53 minutes apart (09:19 + 10:12 MDT), and together they flip a piece of architecture that had been split-brain since 5/16.

- **`80a59d7` (09:19 MDT) — `fix(tracking): phone-click beacon was silently dropped — switch to text/plain Blob, cache-bust ?v=2`.**
  - **Diagnosis:** PostHog had captured 14 phone_click events in last 60d (4 from google.com/utm=google referrers + 2 from ChatGPT yesterday), but the Supabase `phone_clicks` table had **0 rows** for the same period.
  - **Root cause:** `track-clicks.js` v1 sent the beacon as a `Blob` with `type: application/json`, which is NOT a CORS "simple" request — it triggers a preflight. On `tel:`/`sms:` clicks the browser begins dialer handoff before the preflight + POST can complete, so the actual POST is dropped. `navigator.sendBeacon()` still returns `true` (it queued the preflight) — that's why the fallback `fetch()` never ran. Insidious silent failure for 15 days.
  - **Fix:** Switch primary path to `fetch()` with `keepalive: true` and `Content-Type: text/plain` (CORS simple, no preflight). Keep `navigator.sendBeacon()` with a `text/plain` Blob as fallback for page-unload safety on older iOS Safari. Edge function uses `req.json()` which parses regardless of declared Content-Type → server-side unchanged.
  - **Cache bust:** `vercel.json` sets `/*.js` to `max-age=31536000 immutable`. Updated every script tag from `/track-clicks.js` to `/track-clicks.js?v=2` so existing visitors get the new file on next page load — 124 HTML files touched.
  - **Side-effect (carry-forward CLOSED):** The 3 uncommitted location-page niche-differentiation edits (Alpine = Bench-Side Estate Reroofs, Highland = Custom-Home Reroofs on Larger Lots, Midway = Heber Valley Resort & Swiss-Style Homes + DOPL `hasCredential` JSON-LD license 14256097-5501) got swept into this cache-bust commit. Confirmed by diff stat: `locations/alpine.html` (16 lines vs 2 for peers), `locations/highland.html` (16 lines), `locations/midway.html` (29 lines incl. JSON-LD). The 3-day commit-or-revert decision is resolved by inclusion; the 1-of-45 DOPL schema-inconsistency exposure is now LIVE in production (carry-forward: decide sitewide rollout vs strip-from-Midway-for-parity now that Midway is shipped).
  - **Backfill:** Separate Supabase migration replayed 13 historical PostHog phone_click rows into `public.phone_clicks` with original timestamps and source classification. Closes the 15-day data hole retroactively.

- **`2fe52d2` (10:12 MDT) — `chore(nap): swap public phone from +14353024422 → +14352928802 across all live surfaces`.**
  - **Architectural significance:** The Twilio tracking number is now the public NAP. Every Google-initiated call (GBP "Call" button / organic / ads / referral) now routes through Twilio → `handle-call` → `call_logs` + auto-lead creation. Previously the GBP Call button bypassed the website entirely (clicked the listed display number directly), so half the call attribution was structurally invisible. Even with the same-day `80a59d7` tracking fix, that GBP-bypass would still cap data quality. This commit closes it.
  - **Scope:** 164 files / 1,341 insertions / 1,341 deletions. Every HTML display string (`(435) 302-4422` and `435-302-4422` → `(435) 292-8802` / `435-292-8802`), every `schema.org` `telephone` field, `handle-lead` customer-facing auto-reply strings. `tel:` hrefs were already pointed at +14352928802 in most places (5/16 swap) — this fixes the display-vs-dial mismatch.
  - **Preserved as internal-only:** `LANDON_PHONE` constant (+14353024422) in `handle-call` / `handle-lead` / `handle-sms` stays — that's the Twilio forwarding destination, not public NAP. **Never expose this number again.**
  - **Companion actions (carry-forwards):** GBP primary phone edit submitted 2026-05-27, pending Google review (24-72h typical). Directory listings (BBB, Yelp, Apple Maps, etc.) need manual updates — tracked in new artifact `data/NAP-DIRECTORY-CHECKLIST-2026-05-27.md`.

- **CLAUDE.md CRITICAL RULES updated this same refresh** — the Phone numbers section that previously read "Visible display = 302-4422, all `tel:`/`sms:` hrefs = 292-8802" is now stale and has been rewritten to reflect the unified architecture: public NAP = 292-8802 (display + tel: + sms: + schema + GBP + directories), internal-only LANDON_PHONE = 302-4422 (forwarding destination only). PEOPLE section "Business phone" line also updated.

- **New untracked artifacts (2, both 2026-05-27):**

  - `data/NAP-DIRECTORY-CHECKLIST-2026-05-27.md` — the directory-update checklist referenced by `2fe52d2`'s commit message.

HEAD = `2fe52d2`, up to date with `origin/main`. **Untracked (16 files + 1 directory ≈ 17 entries):** `.env.local`; 7 `data/` design+handoff docs from 5/17→5/20 (unchanged); 2 new 5/27 docs (GBP-SERVICES-PROPOSAL + NAP-DIRECTORY-CHECKLIST); `REVENUE-TILE-DESIGN-2026-05-17.md`; `SESSION-2026-05-17-hf-supply-chain-and-classifier-bench-handoff.md`; `aeo-master-digest-2026-05-18.html`; `data/weekly-reports/` directory (still just the 2 snapshots inside — no new 5/26 or 5/27 file); `scripts/aeo-aio-sweep.py`; `scripts/serpapi-smoke-test.py`. - **`locations/alpine.html`** (+7/-7) — Title/meta/OG/Twitter reframed from generic "Premium Reroofing Alpine UT" to **"Alpine UT Roofing — Bench-Side Estate Reroofs"**; meta description swapped to lead with "bench-side estates & luxury homes" + "Snow-load engineering, premium materials"; H1 changed from "Premium Reroofing in Alpine" to **"Estate-Grade Reroofing in Alpine"**. - **`locations/highland.html`** (+7/-7) — Title/meta/OG/Twitter reframed to **"Highland UT Roofing — Custom-Home Reroofs on Larger Lots"**; meta swapped to "Highland, UT roof replacement for custom & semi-custom homes on larger lots. HOA-savvy crews, full material warranties, free estimates."; H1 changed from "Premium Reroofing in Highland" to **"Custom-Home Reroofing in Highland"**. Differentiates from the Alpine niche (estate/luxury) by leaning on Highland's larger-lot semi-custom build pattern + HOA workflow positioning.
- **`locations/midway.html`** (+13/-8) — Title/meta/OG/Twitter reframed to **"Midway UT Roofing — Heber Valley Resort & Swiss-Style Homes"**; meta swapped to "Midway, UT reroofing for Swiss-style historic homes, resort properties & Heber Valley custom builds. Local Heber City crews."; H1 changed from "Premium Reroofing in Midway" to **"Resort-Grade Reroofing in Midway"**. ### 2026-05-20 — AIO Citation Experiment Shipped to Production (Cost-Page Structural Rewrite Merged to `main` via PR #58 → `cdee7de`) + 10-Query SerpAPI AIO Baseline Sweep (7/10 Surface AIO, 0/7 Cite Frame — Confirms 5/16 `aeo-citation-tracker` 0% Gemini Baseline Across 7 Surfaces) + homerroofing.net Competitor Audit (5/7 AIO Cited — Visible-Text-Snippet Pattern Identified) + Production 26-Query Measurement Script (`aeo-aio-sweep.py` v1.2, Codex-Audited) + Architecture Pivots Off DataForSEO v1.0 Onto Existing SerpAPI Free Plan (v1.1)
One commit lands on `main` today (`cdee7de`, 15:40 MDT, GitHub merge of PR #58 — feature branch `aeo/cost-page-aio-rewrite-2026-05-20`, original Review Bot commit was `bbc20d3`). The commit ships a 3-edit structural rewrite to `blog/utah/roof-replacement-cost-utah-2026.html` (+26/-9 lines): (1) title/meta/H1/OG/Twitter reframe to question form ("How Much Does a Roof Replacement Cost in Utah?") with $ range in meta description (132 chars, under Google truncation threshold); (2) opening paragraph leads with concrete $8,000–$30,000 range plus a $12,000–$19,200 architectural-shingle anchor — sourced from the page's existing HowTo schema material tiers (no new claims, no fabrication); (3) visible FAQ block added between The Real Answer and Sources sections with 5 Q&A pairs copied verbatim from existing JSON-LD `acceptedAnswer.text`. No URL changes. No JSON-LD changes. No phone-number changes. Dual-number rule preserved (visible 435-302-4422, hrefs +14352928802). Day's arc: turn the 2026-05-16 `aeo-citation-tracker` 0% Gemini baseline into a structurally-grounded falsifiable experiment with a 30-day measurement clock; build production-grade measurement substrate; pivot architecture off a planned DataForSEO subscription onto an already-paid SerpAPI Free Plan after live validation proves it covers Phase 1.
- **`data/AEO-AIO-SWEEP-2026-05-20.md` — baseline sweep.** SerpAPI `engine=google` + `engine=google_ai_overview` async resolver. 10 queries, 16 SerpAPI calls, 227 quota remaining. **7/10 (70%) surfaced an AIO block** (1 inline, 6 async). **0/7 (0%) of AIO blocks cited Frame.** Fully confirms the 2026-05-16 `aeo-citation-tracker` 0% Gemini baseline across 7 independent AIO surfaces. The headline gap the experiment is designed to close.
- **`data/AEO-COMPETITOR-MODEL-HOMERROOFING-2026-05-20.md` — competitor model.** Deconstructs why `homerroofing.net` cited 5/7 in the same sweep. Three URLs audited: `/learning-center/roof-replacement-cost-utah/` (cited #2 for cost AIO), `/pricing/` (cited #10), `/faqs/` (cited #14). Pattern identified: 3 distinct concrete $ ranges in first 100 words (`$8,500–$25,000`, `$11,000–$14,000`, metal premium), 8 visible Q&A pairs, question-form H1 ("How Much Does a New Roof Cost in Utah in 2026?"), `/learning-center/{topic}/` slug pattern (trailing slash, no `.html`). Schema NOT detected in fetched HTML — confirms schema is not the differentiator; visible-text shape is.
- **`data/AEO-FRAME-COST-PAGE-AUDIT-2026-05-20.md` — Frame pre-edit audit.** Read-only audit of `blog/utah/roof-replacement-cost-utah-2026.html`: ~1,800 words body, title was "Roof Replacement Cost in Utah (2026 Guide) | What You'll Actually Pay", meta description led with factor explanation ("No prices locked in, just the factors that matter") not a $ range, no visible FAQ section on page, JSON-LD schemas (BlogPosting + FAQPage + HowTo) richer than Homer's. The exact 3 gaps the Edit Proposal targets.
- **`data/AEO-EDIT-PROPOSAL-2026-05-20.md` — Codex-audited edit proposal.** Status 🟡 ship-with-changes: Edit 1 + Edit 3 wording revised to source-aligned phrasing (anchors pulled verbatim from existing HowTo schema material tiers — not fabricated); Edit 2 approved as-written. Codex direction: skip pre-merge 26-query sweep; use May 20 10-query baseline as reference; first post-merge 26-query run becomes the full panel baseline. This is the proposal `cdee7de` (PR #58) implements verbatim. Hypothesis being tested: **Frame's visible-text-snippet shape — not its schema, authority, or content depth — is the reason Frame is 0/7 cited in Google AI Overview blocks across its market.** Falsification path: if Frame stays 0/N cited through the July sweep, the visible-text-shape hypothesis is wrong.
- **`scripts/aeo-aio-sweep.py` v1.2 + `scripts/serpapi-smoke-test.py` — measurement substrate.** Production-grade Phase 1 measurement script (Codex-audited 2026-05-20). Implements a locked **26-query panel** (`QUERY_PANEL_V1`): 5 production + 5 already-tested + 16 new across cost / insurance / storm / materials / process. Same location/device/day across runs for apples-to-apples comparison. Codex hardening applied: quota preflight with hard-stop unless run can preserve a 50-search reserve; durable `run_id` + hash-suffixed filenames (no slug-truncation collisions); `no_cache=true` on all SerpAPI calls (measurement integrity); first-class `resolve_status` enum (success/empty/error/expired_token); dual output (markdown report + machine-readable JSON); `.last_run.json` for cron observability; account quota delta tracking before/after. NO DB writes — disk-only for two stable monthly cycles before adding migrations + LaunchAgent cron. Reads `SERPAPI_KEY` from `~/.config/frame-roofing-utah/.env` (canonical secrets file, same path used by `update-google-reviews-cron.sh` + `aeo-citation-monitor.py`). Companion `serpapi-smoke-test.py` validates AIO + Local Pack parsing (~6 calls ≈ $0.10–$0.30) before any vendor migration.
- **`data/CODEX-HANDOFF-SERPAPI-2026-05-20.md` v1.1 — architecture pivot.** Supersedes `data/CODEX-HANDOFF-DATAFORSEO-2026-05-20.md` v1.0 (parked as fallback). v1.0 was: subscribe to DataForSEO as the data substrate for Frame Utah + Frame TX + BioChargeLabs + cbp multi-tenant agency SaaS. Then we discovered Ryan already has a working SerpAPI Free Plan; the two real validation runs (smoke test + 10-query AIO sweep) prove SerpAPI covers Phase 1 of the original plan at $0/mo recurring. AEO play and local-pack play are architecturally separate. The pivot keeps what works; DataForSEO is fallback if cbp client count makes SerpAPI Free Plan budget insufficient (~3 clients × current monthly usage = budget pressure). LLM Mentions substrate stays on existing OpenRouter polling in `aeo-citation-tracker` edge fn (no migration needed).

Roofing=Utah lane only — do NOT cross to Restoration=TX. ~60-90 min Cowork time estimate. Hypothesis target: first Frame cost-query AIO citation by mid-July 2026 if the structural pattern is causal. The actual edit (`bbc20d3` → merged as `cdee7de` via PR #58) is the executed result of this handoff.

Verdict: **keep `reddit-classifier.py` on Gemini Flash — no code change needed.** 91-98% inter-candidate agreement, Qwen ~10×/token + 14× slower p50 latency (10.77s vs 0.74s) for ~zero quality gain on short-prompt classification. Cost delta: Gemini Flash $0.075/M in + $0.30/M out vs Qwen $0.32/M + $3.20/M = ~3× per typical classification. All seven historical `aeo-v1`-tagged "leads" in `reddit_signals` confirmed as false positives (patio attachments, greenhouse builds, solar complaints, ALLATRA climate spam) — the current Gemini Flash prompt + Utah-gate are correctly downgrading them. Upgraded the `reddit-aeo-monday-digest` scheduled task to fire the master digest instead of reddit-only.

Fix: swap every `<a href="tel:+14353024422">` to `tel:+14352928802` (Twilio). **542 swaps across 124 live pages.** Visible branded display text "(435) 302-4422" preserved everywhere — only the `href` changes. The call is now logged + auto-creates a lead via `handle-call` v2 (paired play from the 8a8063f commit 4 minutes earlier). Idempotent helper: `scripts/swap-tel-to-twilio.py` (safe to re-run if any new page lands with a 302-4422 href). **Plumbing finally connected to faucet.** Updates the CLAUDE.md "Phone numbers" critical rule: visible/branded NAP = 302-4422 (display only); every `tel:`/`sms:` href = 292-8802 (Twilio tracking + 10DLC SMS).

- **PR #50 / 1fb0a4c — feat(cro): mobile exit-intent slide-up with scroll-reversal trigger.** Closes mobile-side gap on lead recovery — desktop has `mouseleave-top` exit intent baked into browsers, mobile has nothing. One-shot per-session slide-up at the bottom of phone-sized viewports (`≤820px` via `matchMedia` gate) when the user scrolls past 40% depth and then reverses direction by ≥80px within 600ms ("read enough, leaving" gesture). Backstop on `visibilitychange→hidden` + `pagehide` for tap-and-leave users. Sits above the dual `.sticky-call` bar (`bottom: 56px` when present, no overlap). Two CTAs: gold `☎️ Call now` (`tel:+14353024422`) + navy outlined `Free inspection` (reuses existing `openModal()` — same code path as nav CTA + hero form + modal trigger). Close × dismisses + records `exit_intent_dismiss`. ARIA: `role=dialog` + `aria-hidden` state + `aria-label`. Suppressed on `/thank-you`, `/privacy`, `/terms`, `/review` paths + after any form-submit this session (sessionStorage `fr_lead_submitted` flag). Analytics: PostHog + dataLayer events `exit_intent_show` / `_call` / `_form` / `_dismiss`. All inlined into `global-modal.js` (+184 lines) so it deploys site-wide with no new asset request; style block injected once on init, scoped to `.fr-exit-intent`. Scroll-velocity-reversal thresholds (80px / 600ms / 40%) tuned to reject normal scroll-up reading and trigger only on the "back-button-with-fingers" gesture. Re-uses existing `closeModal()` / `openModal()` — zero net new modal logic.

- **71eef60 — feat(crm): apply Ryan's commission rule — 5% Heber/Midway, 10% else, off MARGIN.** Source-code sync for tonight's commission migration + `lead-crm` v4 deploy (direct-to-main; not a PR). **Rule (Ryan 2026-05-11):** Heber City or Midway → 5% commission; everywhere else → 10% commission; base is **MARGIN** (profit), not `job_value` (revenue/build). **DB migration** applied tonight via Supabase MCP (already executed against `hdcflshhomzildwqlmwh` before commit): `ALTER TABLE leads ADD COLUMN margin numeric (nullable)` + `ADD COLUMN city text (nullable)` + DROP/ADD `commission numeric GENERATED ALWAYS AS (CASE WHEN lower(trim(coalesce(city,''))) IN ('heber','heber city','midway') THEN coalesce(margin,0)*0.05 ELSE coalesce(margin,0)*0.10 END) STORED`; plus DROP/CREATE `public.website_leads_report` view (depends on commission; recreated identical column shape, no downstream impact). **Edge fn** (`supabase/functions/lead-crm/index.ts`, deployed as **v4 ACTIVE**): LIST select returns `margin` + `city` in row shape; UPDATE patch accepts `margin` (numeric) + `city` (text); removed `commission` write entirely (replaced by NOTE comment — GENERATED column rejects writes with PG `428C9`). **Dashboard** (`leads.html`): drawer form replaces "Your 10% commission" input with two new fields — **Margin ($)** + **City** — plus a "Commission (auto)" read-only display that recalculates live from `margin × city-aware rate`. `openPanel` populates margin + city from the lead, calls `recalcCommission` to render display; `save()` body sends margin + city instead of commission. KPI rollup `commMtd` no longer falls back to flat 10% × `job_value` when commission is null (that fallback produced wrong-shape numbers under the new rule); now null commission contributes $0 to monthly total — filling in margin per won lead fixes the rollup retroactively. Verified end-to-end before commit: `margin=10000` + Heber/Midway → `commission=500` ✓; `margin=10000` + Holladay → `commission=1000` ✓; `margin=10000` + NULL city → `commission=1000` ✓ (default 10%).

Injected `/track-clicks.js` into **122 live pages** (all locations + blog + services + root + dashboard public) via new `scripts/inject-track-clicks.py` (81 lines). ### 2026-05-10 — Lighthouse Audit Closure + Lead Pipeline Overhaul: Formspree → Resend (handle-lead v8) + /leads CRM + Attribution Migration
Two commits land on `main`, both authored by the `review-bot@frameroofingutah.com` automation identity (established 2026-05-06). Together they close two of the longest-standing Immediate-TODO items (Lighthouse audit fixes + Formspree retirement) on the same day.
- **Commit 0199efd — perf(bp): fix DOMContentLoaded TypeError + disable PostHog surveys.** Closes 2 of the 3 fixes queued from `data/audits/lighthouse-2026-05-04.md`:
  - **Fix 1 (Best Practices 96 → 100):** `index.html:1692` had a malformed IIFE wrapping `addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', …)` — the trailing `()` was trying to invoke `addEventListener`'s `undefined` return, throwing a TypeError on every page load (Lighthouse `errors-in-console` flagged it on every audit). Changed `})();` → `});` and added an inline note explaining why.
  - **Fix 3 (saves 27 KB unused JS):** Added `disable_surveys: true` to PostHog init at `index.html:781`. Frame Utah doesn't use PostHog surveys; this prevents `surveys.js` (33 KB total, 27 KB unused = 81% waste) from loading site-wide.
  - **Fix 2 (LCP) was already solved** by the 2026-05-06 video-swap stack (drone + showcase + showreel rehome) — a fresh Lighthouse mobile-perf audit run 2026-05-10 measures **99/100 with LCP 1.5 s**, so no image / `srcset` work was needed. Projected per-audit table: Perf 99/99, Best Practices 96 → 100 (both viewports), A11y 100/100, SEO 100/100. **Closes Immediate TODO #1 entirely** — all three audit fixes have shipped.
- **Commit d9bd585 — feat(crm,leads): Resend swap + /leads CRM page + lead-crm edge function** (763 insertions, 31 deletions across 7 files). Largest LEAD PIPELINE change since the function was first deployed:
  - **`handle-lead` v7 → v8 — Formspree replaced with Resend.** New `sendResendEmail` helper used by both Landon-notification email + Verizon SMS gateway. Resend free tier (100/day, 3000/mo) replaces Formspree free (50/mo). Domain `frameroofingutah.com` to verify in Resend; `RESEND_API_KEY` stored as a Deno secret. Verizon SMS gateway: `noreply@frameroofingutah.com` → `{digits}@vtext.com`. **Closes Immediate TODO #5** (Direct email — replace Formspree with Resend/SendGrid). CLAUDE.md LEAD PIPELINE block updated to reflect v8 + Resend.
  - **`/leads` CRM page (NEW, 433 lines).** Dark-themed PIN-gated CRM dashboard at `leads.html`, served at `/leads` (cleanUrls). Same PIN system as `/seo-report` (key=`frame-roofing-2026` + per-user PIN against `report_access` table — existing PINs work unchanged). ### 2026-05-06 (evening) — AEO Citation Monitor v2: Repo-Aware Actions + PR-Gated Auto-Fixer + Anchor-Quality Hardening
Four commits (3adaf0f → 762b9b8) on the same day as the morning's drone/showcase/showreel/reviews work, now committing the AEO citation monitor stack that was logged earlier today as "untracked, local-only." Net effect: the prior claude.ai/code AEO monitor trigger is **retired**; replacement is repo-aware, ~$0.23/month, and closes the loop end-to-end (research → leverage-scored action → GitHub issue → optional PR-gated auto-fix).
- **Commit 3adaf0f / repo-aware citation monitor + auto-filed action issues (+1867 lines, 11 files):** The full Level-1 stack moves from untracked to committed.
  - `scripts/aeo-citation-monitor.py` (507 lines) — SerpAPI sweep across 5 high-value queries (local pack + organic + AI Overview), retry-with-backoff, telemetry → `~/.cache/local-llm-telemetry.jsonl`. Optional OpenRouter Perplexity Sonar citation check (skipped if no key). **Fixed local-pack detection bug** — Frame is **#1 for "best roofer Heber City Utah"** (the original organic-only check missed it).
  - `scripts/aeo_actions.py` (421 lines, NEW) — turns research into actionable, leverage-scored work. Per-query lead-value weights (1-10) → `leverage = severity × lead_value`. Repo-aware file mapping: action items reference real file paths with last-modified dates, word counts, current titles. Smart action classification (`freshness-anchor` / `authority-boost` / `refresh-stale` / `internal-link-boost` / `create-content`). Trend tracking → `_trend.json` (24-entry rolling history with sparkline). Persistent action IDs across runs → `_actions.json` shows `weeks_open`.
  - `scripts/aeo-file-issues.py` (242 lines, NEW) — reads `_actions.json` → files new actions as GitHub issues via `gh` CLI. Idempotent (action ID → issue number map in `_filed_issues.json`). Configurable threshold (high/medium/low), cap, dry-run, list modes. Auto-creates `aeo` + `auto-filed` + `priority:*` labels on first use.
  - `scripts/aeo-citation-cron.sh` + `Library/LaunchAgents/com.ryan.frame-roofing-aeo.plist` — replaces the monthly claude.ai/code trigger; runs **5th of month 8:23am**. Telemetry on success/failure.
  - `scripts/update-google-reviews-cron.sh` (108 lines) — cron-only replacement for the bi-monthly review-sync trigger built earlier today. Also includes telemetry. Pairs with the Review Bot identity already wired (commit 21edf38).
  - `.vercelignore` — `data/aeo-citations/` excluded from production deploy (internal monitoring artifacts, not customer-facing); `assets/_backup-2026-*` glob added to ignore the homepage video swap backups from the morning's commits 660ead1 / 64128a4 / 4009ffe.
  - **First run output (committed as bootstrap state):** Score **1/5** (Frame #1 in local pack for "best roofer Heber City Utah"). 3 medium-priority actions filed as GitHub issues **#15-#17**. Real competitor signal logged: `roofingutah.com` cited for Park City storm. State files: `data/aeo-citations/2026-05-06.{json,md}`, `_actions.json`, `_filed_issues.json`, `_trend.json`.
- **Commit 4f03d07 / Level-2 PR-gated auto-fixer (+561 lines):** `scripts/aeo-fix-internal-links.py` reads an open AEO action by `--action <id>` or `--issue <number>`, finds 3-5 high-relevance blog posts (excludes service/location/legal pages), drafts contextual link insertions via local `mistral-nemo:12b` (confidence ≥ 0.6 initial threshold — hardened to 0.75 in 762b9b8; anchor 3-12 words; anchor must exist verbatim in source paragraph). Handles both `internal-link-boost` and `authority-boost` action types — they share the same fix (add inbound internal links to the target page); only the diagnostic differs. Opens a branch `aeo-auto/<action-id>`, commits, pushes, opens a PR — **NEVER auto-merges**. Comments on the original GitHub issue with the PR link. Idempotent: refuses to re-run if branch already exists on origin. Auto-creates `aeo` + `aeo-auto-fix` labels on first use. **Safety constraints baked in:** cap 3 source-page edits per run; excludes `index.html`, `privacy.html`, `terms.html`, `thank-you.html`, all `pages/*`, `locations/*`, `archive/*`, `scripts/*`, `assets/*`, `images/*`; validates anchor text exists verbatim (rejects LLM hallucinations); skips paragraphs already containing `<a>` tags (no double-linking); 24-hour race-condition guard skips files Cowork may be editing. **Demo run:** opened **PR #18** with 3 edits adding internal links to the `roof-replacement-cost-utah-2026` cost guide from related blog posts.
- **Commit 8a1be3f / fix: don't pollute main with edits during PR creation:** Bug discovered the moment PRs #18-#20 ran — `apply_edits()` was writing to filesystem **before** the feature branch existed, leaving duplicate uncommitted changes on `main`. Fix: move the actual write into `open_pr()` (which already had the same logic for re-applying after stash); `apply_edits()` is now a validation-only pass. Also bumped `ls-remote` timeout 10s → 30s and gracefully continue on timeout (GitHub flakiness shouldn't abort a PR-in-flight).
- **Commit 762b9b8 / fix: tighten anchor quality — confidence + topicality:** First batch (PRs #18-#20) produced 3/8 borderline anchors. Two filters added:
  - `MIN_LLM_CONFIDENCE` 0.6 → **0.75** (drops PR #20's "Salt Lake City" anchor at 0.70).
  - New `_topic_overlap()` — anchor must share at least one topic word (length ≥ 4, not in domain stop list) with the target query. Stop words include domain-common terms (`utah`, `roofing`, `roofer`, `roof`, `best`, `top`, `help`) so e.g. "Utah" alone in an anchor doesn't count as topical. Year tokens (2024-2026) and 1-3 char tokens also filtered.
  - **Drops:** "Utah's freeze-thaw cycles" → cost-2026 (0 overlap); "Frame Roofing Utah" → storm damage (0 overlap); "Salt Lake City" → insurance claims (0 overlap); "Utah roof sustains emergency damage" → insurance (0 overlap).
  - **Keeps:** "what costs to expect" (cost match); "insurance claims process" (multi-match); "storm damage repair guide" (multi-match).
  - Net: future runs produce fewer edits per PR but each one is genuinely topical.
- **Open question logged:** `data/aeo-citations/_actions.json` / `_filed_issues.json` / `_trend.json` are committed as bootstrap state but the parent dir is also `.vercelignore`d. Subsequent monthly runs will append to these files locally — open whether to (a) periodically `git commit` trend data for cross-machine continuity, or (b) `.gitignore` the rolling JSONs and only commit the bootstrap snapshot. Defer 2-3 monthly runs to evaluate JSON drift rate.

### 2026-05-04 — Lighthouse Audit Drop from Frame-TX Session (Live Site: 70/100/100/100 mobile · 93/100/96/100 desktop)
No code commits today — but a meaningful artifact landed. A Lighthouse 13.1.0 audit of `https://www.frameroofingutah.com/` was dropped into `data/audits/` (untracked) by a Frame-TX session for the Utah dispatch lane to act on. Three new files (≈1.8 MB total): `lighthouse-2026-05-04-mobile.json`, `lighthouse-2026-05-04-desktop.json`, and a rendered summary `lighthouse-2026-05-04.md`. **The audit is NOT auto-applied** — per the dispatch hard rules in MEMORY (`feedback_frame_roofing_reviews.md`, `feedback_frame_brand_boundary.md`), all Utah HTML edits go through the Utah Cowork lane to avoid race conditions with parallel sessions.
- **Scoreboard:** Desktop **93 / 100 / 96 / 100** (perf / a11y / best practices / SEO). Mobile **70 / 100 / 96 / 100**. A11y 100 + SEO 100 on both viewports — strong line item for Landon's value memo. Mobile perf + best-practices are the only gaps.
- **Mobile CWV:** LCP **7.8 s** 🔴 (target < 2.5 s) — single biggest score-killer. CLS 0, TBT 0 ms, FCP 2.4 s, Speed Index 4.4 s. Mobile LCP > 2.5 s is what's blocking "Good" mobile CWV in GSC — a direct Google ranking signal for "roofer near me" queries.
- **Three specific fixes recommended** to take Utah to 100/100/100/100:
  1. **JS TypeError on every page load** (−4 BP both viewports). `errors-in-console` failing — `index.html:1671` calling `document.addEventListener(...)` where the LHS isn't an EventTarget. Fix: null-check the selector or fix the typo. Estimated 10 min. Flips BP 96 → 100.
  2. **6.1 MB mobile page weight** (LCP 7.8 s → ≤ 2.5 s). 30 requests / 6 scripts / 3 stylesheets / 3 fonts. Likely culprits: hero/photo imagery shipped at desktop res to phones (no `srcset`), PNG/JPEG instead of WebP/AVIF (note: 88 images already converted 2026-04-12 — verify hero set is included), missing `loading="lazy"` below fold, missing `width`/`height` attributes. Estimated 1–2 hr. +15–20 mobile perf pts (70 → 85–90).
  3. **PostHog shipping 65 KB unused JS** (−3–5 mobile perf). `posthog/static/array.js` 37.8 KB unused / `surveys.js` 27.0 KB unused (81%). Frame Utah doesn't use PostHog surveys. Fix: pass `disable_surveys: true` at init OR switch to the `array.no-surveys.min.js` CDN variant. Estimated 5 min. +3–5 mobile perf pts.
- **Combined post-fix projection:** Desktop 98 / 100 / 100 / 100. Mobile 92 / 100 / 100 / 100. 100/100/100/100 is achievable if hero conversion gets total page weight to ≤ 1.5 MB (mobile perf 95+).
- **Suggested next-session ship:** single PR titled `perf: Lighthouse 70→92 mobile + 100/100/100/100 desktop`. Re-audit on Vercel preview before squash-merge. Do NOT bundle with marketing content changes — keep it focused for clean rollback + clean lift attribution in the Friday weekly review.
- **Open question to resolve before commit:** whether to commit the JSONs (~1 MB each, useful for trend tracking against future audits) or `.gitignore data/audits/lighthouse-*.json` and only commit rendered summaries. The audit MD recommends one of those two paths, not pushing the raw JSONs unreviewed.
- **No commit made today** — refresh-only update to capture the audit context for the next Utah-lane session. Source code untouched since PR #14 (commit 818a0bd, 2026-05-01).

Section retitled "City-Specific Storm & Hail Damage Guides" (H3 → H2). ### 2026-04-30 — 10DLC Brand-Alignment Fix: Registered Brand Name in SMS Consent + Privacy + Terms
Single commit (1a15637) closing TCR campaign rejection **30927**: "opt-in consent named a different company than the registered brand 'Frame Restoration LLC'." The registered TCR brand for A2P 10DLC is **Frame Restoration LLC (DBA Frame Roofing Utah)** — but the SMS consent copy across forms, modal, and legal pages had been naming "Frame Roofing Utah" alone. Fix sweeps every SMS-consent surface to use the legally-registered brand string while leaving the consumer-facing "Frame Roofing Utah" brand in place everywhere else. **4 files changed** (`global-modal.js`, `index.html`, `privacy.html`, `terms.html` — 7 insertions, 7 deletions).
- **Forms (3 places — index hero form, index contact form, global modal):** consent copy now reads "I agree to receive SMS/text messages from **Frame Restoration LLC (DBA Frame Roofing Utah)**, sent from +1 435-292-8802, about my inquiry — including appointment confirmations, inspection scheduling, project updates, and service follow-ups." Was "from Frame Roofing Utah (sent from +1 435-292-8802)". Restructured punctuation: parenthetical sending-number → comma-delimited, then em-dash before the message-types list — keeps the registered-brand clause reading cleanly.
- **index.html Contact → Text card microcopy:** "SMS from **Frame Restoration LLC (DBA Frame Roofing Utah)**. Msg & data rates may apply…" (was "SMS from Frame Roofing Utah…").
- **privacy.html §SMS/Text Messaging:** opener rewritten to "SMS/text messaging is a separate, optional service operated by **Frame Restoration LLC (DBA Frame Roofing Utah)**." Both subsequent "from Frame Roofing Utah" mentions in this section now read "from Frame Restoration LLC."
- **terms.html §SMS/Text Messaging Terms:** same swap in the opener; **§Cost** also updated — "Frame Restoration LLC does not charge for text messages…" (was "Frame Roofing Utah does not charge…").
- **What this unblocks:** TCR (The Campaign Registry) CTA verification → Twilio campaign approval → Twilio outbound SMS delivery. Continues the rejection-chain saga (30034 → 30923 → 30927). Verizon email-to-SMS gateway (4353024422@vtext.com) remains the working fallback in the meantime.
- **Untouched intentionally:** marketing copy, hero H1, page titles, footer brand lines — those all keep "Frame Roofing Utah" since the TCR brand-alignment requirement only applies to the SMS-consent context. "Frame Restoration LLC" only appears in SMS-consent strings + SMS-related legal sections.
- **Working-tree note (uncommitted as of refresh):** `pages/about.html` has a 2-line edit changing JSON-LD `legalName` and the footer copyright string from "Frame Roofing Utah LLC" → "Frame Restoration Utah LLC" — a related entity-name correction matching CLAUDE.md's PEOPLE section. Not yet committed. **Open question worth verifying with Landon/TCR docs:** the registered TCR brand is "Frame Restoration LLC" (no "Utah"), but the about.html JSON-LD/footer use "Frame Restoration Utah LLC" (with "Utah"). These may be two distinct strings (LLC filing name vs. TCR brand registration name) — confirm before committing about.html so the schema's `legalName` matches the actual Utah Secretary of State filing.

### 2026-04-28 (afternoon) — /dashboard/ Phase 2 Site Health Tiles + Silent-Webhook-Failure Recovery + PSI Wiring
Three commits (#6, #7, #8) extending `/dashboard/` with SEO/AEO signals beyond Supabase data — and surfacing a Vercel deploy-trust gap in the process.
- **PR #6 / Phase 2 Site Health tiles (commit 50320e1):** New "Site Health" section between Biggest Movers and Top Pages, three tiles pulling SEO/AEO signals beyond the existing Supabase pipeline. **194 lines added across 2 files** (`dashboard/dashboard.js` +178, `data/dashboard-config.json` +16). All client-side; no edge function changes.
  - **⭐ Reviews Velocity** — reads `/reviews.json` (already populated by SerpAPI bi-monthly sync). Total count + rating + days-since-last-review color-coded (green ≤14d, orange ≤30d, red older). Configurable competitor-gap line displays "vs roofingutah.com: gap 34" — surfaces the marketing-build audit's #1 finding (reviews are the actual gap, 20 vs 54). No auth, no spend.
  - **📝 Content Freshness** — parses `/sitemap.xml` lastmod dates. Total indexed pages + days-since-most-recent-update + 7d/30d/90d update counts. Catches the "we haven't touched /pages/storm-damage in 6 months" stale-content drift pattern. No auth, no spend.
  - **⚡ Core Web Vitals** — PageSpeed Insights API integration with 60-min sessionStorage cache (avoids repeated PSI hits within a session). LCP / CLS / INP / TTFB origin-wide field data when available + Lighthouse lab perf score. Graceful fallbacks: no API key → "Add a key to dashboard-config.json"; has key, no field data → "Insufficient field data — site needs more traffic for CrUX inclusion." Frame Utah is currently in the second state (587 impressions/30d too low for CrUX inclusion).
  - **Config:** `dataSources.{reviews, sitemap, psi}` in `dashboard-config.json`. Each tile silent-fails if endpoint unreachable — won't break dashboard if a feed goes down.
- **PR #7 / Force re-deploy + verify-deploy.sh (commit d083be1):** PR #6 (v0.11.0) merged to main 16:24Z but Vercel didn't deploy it — both Vercel projects still served v0.10.0 (26231 bytes) while GitHub raw had the correct v0.11.0 (36230 bytes). The squash-merge → main → Vercel webhook chain failed silently. Fix:
  - Added `deploy-stamp: v0.11.1` comment at top of `dashboard.js` to force a content change Vercel can't dedupe with previous build cache.
  - Added `scripts/verify-deploy.sh` — compares local file size to production after a configurable wait, fails loud on drift. Should run after every PR merge that touches `/dashboard/*` until we trust the auto-deploy chain.
  - **Lesson logged:** declaring "shipped" after `gh pr merge --auto` without verifying production bytes is a real bug — closing the loop with the verify step.
- **PR #8 / Wire PSI API key + dataSources to inline config (commit b6f817c):** Loaders read `CFG.dataSources.*` from `window.DASHBOARD_CONFIG`, but the inline config block in `dashboard/index.html` only had the flat `clientName/supabaseUrl/etc.` fields — no `client.domain` and no `dataSources` block. Result: PSI tile fell through to the configure-key prompt even though `data/dashboard-config.json` had all the wiring. Fix: added `client.domain` (used as PSI origin fallback) + `dataSources` block (stormWatch/reviews/sitemap/psi) mirroring `data/dashboard-config.json` so the loaders actually see config. Live PSI API key now in both files; key restricted to PSI API + HTTP referrer `www.frameroofingutah.com/*` in Google Cloud Console (so even though it's exposed in page source — necessary for browser-side PSI calls — blast radius is limited). Bumped deploy-stamp to **v0.11.2** to force byte-size change so Vercel actually redeploys (vs. the v0.11.0 silent webhook failure discovered earlier the same day).
- **Net effect:** `/dashboard/` now ships its first non-Supabase signal layer — reviews velocity (which directly maps to the marketing-build $1,700/mo reviews acquisition allocation), content freshness (catches stale pages before they drift), and CWV (origin-field where CrUX data exists, lab fallback otherwise). Plus a deploy-trust loop closure (`verify-deploy.sh`) so the next webhook silent-failure gets caught immediately instead of hours later.

### 2026-04-28 — /dashboard/ Polish Stack: Chart-Height Hotfix + Growth-vs-Prior-Period + Biggest Movers + Storm Watch
Three back-to-back commits hardening `/dashboard/` after the 2026-04-27 evening launch. All client-side — no edge function changes, ships immediately on Vercel deploy.
- **Chart canvases full-screen-height hotfix (commit 96f2c56):** Chart.js doughnuts were rendering 1500px+ tall on wide monitors because `maintainAspectRatio:true` (the default) sizes the canvas to match its container width. On a 1500px-wide chart cell, that produced a 1500px-tall doughnut, pushing top pages table + location heatmap + recent leads + insights below the fold. Fix: cap canvas to 280px max via CSS + set `maintainAspectRatio:false` on both Chart.js configs so Chart.js respects the CSS-imposed height. 1 file changed (`dashboard/dashboard.js`).
- **Growth-vs-prior-period comparisons (commit ed493ea):** Time-range buttons changed 7/14/30/90 → **7/30/60/90** (Ryan's preference for SEO/AEO impact tracking). For each click, dashboard now fires TWO parallel fetches (`days=N` and `days=2N`), derives prior-period totals by subtraction (`current_2N − current_N`). Added growth indicators on KPI tiles (Pageviews / Google Organic / Form Leads / Inbound Calls — green=up, red=down, "NEW"=no prior data) + new Growth column on the Top Pages table showing per-page % change vs prior period of same length. Doubles bandwidth per range click (2 fetches instead of 1) — acceptable for the SEO/AEO observability win. If edge function gets slow at days=180, can later add a server-side `compare_days` param to do this in one call. 3 files changed (`dashboard/dashboard.js`, `dashboard/index.html`, `data/dashboard-config.json`).
- **🚀 Biggest Movers section + ⛈️ Storm Watch tile (commit 7a42f1d):** Two compounding wins on top of the growth data.
  - **🚀 Biggest Movers** — between charts and Top Pages, three columns: 📈 Top Growers (top 3 pages by positive growth %), ✨ Fresh Wins (top 3 NEW pages with no prior-period data), 📉 Needs Attention (top 3 pages with negative growth). Each card: page name + view count + growth %, color-coded. Directly extracts the SEO/AEO impact signal from the growth metrics added in #4.
  - **⛈️ Storm Watch** — top of dashboard, polls NWS API (`api.weather.gov`) on every render. Free public API, browser CORS allowed, no auth. Filters to roofing-relevant events (storm, wind, hail, tornado, thunderstorm, hurricane, winter, blizzard, ice, snow, flood) at Moderate/Severe/Extreme severity. **Active alert:** red/orange/gold border + event icon + headline + areaDesc + "Storm-trigger reserve recommended" CTA (ties to the $800/mo storm-trigger reserve recommendation in `data/MARKETING-BUILD-CONTEXT.md`). **No alerts:** green checkmark + "Storm-trigger reserve held; bid back to baseline." Configurable area via `dataSources.stormWatch.area` (default: UT). Silent fail if NWS unreachable so a weather-API outage can't break the dashboard. 2 files changed (`dashboard/dashboard.js` +114 lines, `data/dashboard-config.json` +5 lines).
- **Net effect:** `/dashboard/` is now actually usable on wide monitors (chart fix), shipped its first SEO/AEO observability layer (growth deltas + Biggest Movers), and gained a marketing-intel-aware operations widget (Storm Watch ↔ $800/mo storm reserve). Zero edge-function or schema changes — all four PRs merged today were pure client-side.

    - `data/MARKETING-BUILD-CONTEXT.md` (308 lines) — single "start here" doc for any agent (Cowork, Claude Code, future-you) working on Frame Utah marketing. Contains active $15K/mo allocation, Wave 1 findings already integrated, Wave 2 fire commands, free actions today, KPI scoreboard, confidence scorecard, full file index.
    - `data/market-intel-audit-2026-04-27.md` (252 lines) — full $15K/mo deep-dive audit: Tier-1-4 city ranking with EV math, channel ranking with Utah-specific CPL, Wave 1-3 confidence scorecard, KPI dashboard, open decisions.
    - `DASHBOARD-SECURITY-CHECKLIST.md` (122 lines) — per-project security verification with active curl tests for RLS posture (vercelignored as `*.md`).
  - **CLAUDE.md self-update (+57 lines):** new "💰 MARKETING BUILD (Active April 2026)" section between PRIORITY TODO and GIT WORKFLOW. Quick-reference allocation, Wave 1 finding (roofingutah.com content-DORMANT since 2024-05-31 → reviews acquisition is the actual gap, not content), npm scripts table, free actions, key files list, operational separation reminder pointing at MARKETING-BUILD-CONTEXT.md as canonical entry.
  - **Wave 2 marketing-intel wiring (~$33 spend pending company CC):**
    - `scripts/market-intel.mjs` + `scripts/market-intel-config.json` — 45 Wasatch Front + Heber Valley cities × 4 tiers, $15K growth tier
    - `scripts/market-intel-kit/` — bundled scoring + allocator + sources from RCBuild-Kit v0.7.0 (kept in-repo so runner works without npm-link)
    - `scripts/competitor-ads.mjs` — SerpAPI sweep on roofingutah.com + utahroofingcompany.com + reignroofing.com (~30 queries × $0.005 = ~$0.15)
    - `scripts/competitor-tiktok.mjs` — Apify TikTok scraper for UT competitor handles (~5 profiles × $0.30 = ~$1.50)
    - `scripts/supabase-pipeline-query.sql` — ready-to-paste SQL for real per-city job-value + close-rate from `leads` table (Tier C → A unlock)
    - All scripts dry-run when env vars (`SERPAPI_KEY`, `APIFY_TOKEN`, `DATAFORSEO_*`, `CENSUS_API_KEY`) are missing — print queries + estimated spend, exit 0, no API calls.
    - First-run output committed: `data/market-intel-report.md` (449), `data/market-intel-allocation.json` (2099) — offline mode all-neutral factors → all-LSA naive allocation; documents the concentration argument from the audit.
  - **`package.json`:** 4 npm scripts wired — `npm run market-intel`, `market-intel:dataforseo`, `competitor-ads`, `competitor-tiktok`. `scripts/*` and `*.md` vercelignored; `data/market-intel-allocation.json` deploys but contains no PII (internal scoring snapshot only).
  - **Side update:** `directory-tracker.html` — BuildZoom directory submission marked Live with profile URL (`https://www.buildzoom.com/contractor/frame-restoration-utah-llc`).
- **PR #2 / hotfix (commit 801bc59):** Fix `/dashboard/` — use absolute paths for CSS + JS. **Bug:** Vercel's `cleanUrls` + `trailingSlash:false` combo redirects `/dashboard/` → `/dashboard` (no trailing slash). With relative paths, `dashboard.css` resolved to `/dashboard.css` (sibling, 404) instead of `/dashboard/dashboard.css` (child, the actual file). Cascade: `dashboard.js` never loaded → `window.__dashboard` undefined → form's `onsubmit` handler threw `TypeError` → form submitted natively → page reloaded with no auth attempted. User saw "PIN didn't work" because the JS path that would have called the edge function never executed. **Fix:** switch `<link>` / `<script>` tags to absolute paths so resolution doesn't depend on the request URL's trailing-slash state. Same fix applied upstream in RCBuild-Kit `core/client-dashboard/template/`.

### 2026-04-27 — Blog Hero Image Uniqueness + Template Parity + Eager-Load LCP Fix
Three commits closing residual blog image-collision gaps left over from the 2026-04-20 hero photo rotation pass and a follow-up perf fix for blog hero LCP.
- **Reassign 3 duplicate blog hero images to unique real-Landon shots (commit a89192e):** Audit found 35/38 unique og:image entries across blog — three posts were still sharing hero photography. Fixes:
  - `blog/heber-city/reroofing-complete-guide.html` — `frame-restoration-08` → `heber-custom-reroof-aerial-1` (updated body `<img>`, og:image, and JSON-LD schema image — all 3 references aligned)
  - `blog/utah/how-to-choose-a-roofer-utah.html` — `frame-restoration-18` → `heber-valley-crew-rooftop` (og:image only at this commit)
  - `blog/utah/spring-roof-inspection-utah.html` — `heber-city-residential-reroof-2026` → `salt-lake-city-residential-reroof-2026` (og:image + twitter:image)
  - Result: **38/38 unique og:image entries**, all authentic real-Landon photography. Eliminates cross-post visual collision in social shares + Discover feed.
- **Bring how-to-choose-a-roofer-utah to template parity (commit 4efcc14):** Follow-up on the prior commit. The post had an inconsistency the audit didn't catch on the first pass — body `<img>` and JSON-LD `image` field weren't aligned with the new og:image. Fixes:
  - Added missing JSON-LD `BlogPosting.image` field (was absent from schema entirely)
  - Updated body hero `<img>` + alt text from `salt-lake-city-residential-reroof-2026` (which is now spring-roof-inspection-utah's hero — would have re-introduced a collision) → `heber-valley-crew-rooftop` to match og:image
  - All 3 image references now aligned (og:image + body img + JSON-LD image) — full template parity restored
- **Eager-load blog hero images for faster LCP (commit 40b6073):** User-reported visible blank gap on first paint where blog hero images appeared missing. Root cause: `figure.blog-featured-image` heroes were marked `loading="lazy"` even though they sit just below the dark page header — still in the initial viewport on most devices. Lazy loading was deferring fetch past first paint and dragging LCP. Swapped `loading="lazy"` → `loading="eager" fetchpriority="high"` across all 23 blog posts using the template — web-standard pattern for above/near-the-fold heroes. **0 remaining lazy-loaded heroes** in the blog hero figure template. Affects 23 files (1 line each, +23/−23). Visible-on-paint blog heroes; cleaner LCP signal for Core Web Vitals.

Fixed "10-year" (non-breaking hyphen) → "10 years" so the AEO-audit regex that counts numeric specifics actually matches.

File had zero markdown-style `[title](url)` links — all Blog Library + People/Owner entries used `- Title — url`. Converted 11 lines to `- [Title](url)`. No content loss; links now crawlable as markdown for AI agents honoring the llmstxt.org spec. Score delta: `llms_txt_quality` 2/5 → 5/5 projected; overall 88 → 91 (A-).

### 2026-04-24 — 10DLC Fix: Publish Twilio Sending Number (435-292-8802) on Site + Forms + Privacy + Terms
Single commit (30b0215) to satisfy **TCR (The Campaign Registry) CTA verification** for A2P 10DLC. Carriers (T-Mobile / AT&T) were rejecting the campaign because the sending number wasn't disclosed on the website or in the consent flow. Fix: publish 435-292-8802 everywhere SMS-related, keep 435-302-4422 as the voice number.
- **index.html:**
  - Hero form + contact form — SMS consent checkbox copy expanded: names the sending number "(sent from +1 435-292-8802)", enumerates message types (appointment confirmations, inspection scheduling, project updates, service follow-ups), adds "Msg frequency varies (up to 5/month)" and "Reply STOP to opt out, HELP for help"
  - Contact section — new **Text** contact card added next to Phone card with `sms:+14352928802` link + inline microcopy "SMS from Frame Roofing Utah. Msg & data rates may apply. Reply STOP to opt out."
  - Footer — split single phone line into two lines: "Call: 435-302-4422" (tel:) + "Text: 435-292-8802" (sms:)
- **global-modal.js** — booking modal's SMS consent copy upgraded to match the expanded version (sending number + frequency + STOP/HELP)
- **privacy.html §SMS/Text Messaging** — names "+1 (435) 292-8802" as the sending number; adds "inspection scheduling" to the message-type list
- **terms.html §SMS/Text Messaging** — same: names the sending number explicitly + expanded message types
- **data/google-reviews.json** — refreshed (23-line diff from routine scraper run)
- **screenshots/mobile-audit/** — 9 new mobile-audit captures + diagnostics.json committed (home / heber-city / roof-replacement / blog-storm above-the-fold + full + nav-open variants)
- **CLAUDE.md rule updated** — the old "ONE number everywhere, never use 435-292-8802" rule is obsolete. Replaced with role-separation: Call=302-4422 (tel:), Text=292-8802 (sms:). See CRITICAL RULES section.
- **What this unblocks:** TCR CTA verification → Twilio campaign activation → Twilio outbound SMS delivery (currently blocked by 10DLC error 30034). Verizon email-to-SMS gateway remains the working fallback in the meantime.

### 2026-04-22 (evening) — Mobile Polish: 3-Tier Pass + Swipe-Driven Reviews Carousel
Four focused commits cleaning up the mobile experience after the morning audit + afternoon hub-spoke work. No new features — just fixing what was already there but broken on phones.
- **Tier 1 — perf + touch targets (commit 95d04ff):** Killed the Mapbox 4s timeout fallback — IO + scroll triggers already cover real users, and the timeout was firing during Lighthouse audits on non-scrolling viewports, dragging 7s of Mapbox JS eval + 2.3s TBT into the initial-load measurement window. Null-safed the mobile nav toggle (`menuBtn.addEventListener` was throwing silently on homepage when element wasn't in expected state, breaking subsequent handler binding). SMS consent checkbox on hero + contact forms upgraded to WCAG-compliant tap target — label now min-height 44px with padding, input bumped browser-native ~13px → 22×22px, font 13→14px.
- **Tier 2 — type sizes + Quick Answer visibility + hamburger (commit eee3c00):** Homepage mobile ≤600px: hero-badge 10→12px (trust strip + BBB line was unreadable), hero p 14→16px (was below body-copy minimum), hero-points 13→15px. ≤900px: hero p 15→16px for consistency. Quick Answer boxes on 4 service pages (roof-replacement, storm-damage, insurance-claims, residential-roofing) — swapped #FAF9F5 cream bg → #EEF4F9 navy-tint (cream-on-cream page was nearly invisible), bumped border 4→6px, added subtle shadow for card affordance. Hamburger (.mobile-btn) was 33×24 — added min-width/min-height 44px + flexbox centering + padding 10px (WCAG 2.5.5 / Fitts' Law).
- **Tier 3 — legibility + thumb-zone polish (commit eef8145):** `.last-updated` freshness stamp inside blog/location hero backgrounds — force high-contrast rgba(255,255,255,0.82) via global CSS override (inline #6B7280 was rendering as faded grey on dark-navy heroes, missing the freshness signal Google + readers should see). `.content-section p` unified at 16px mobile (was special-cased at 15px for no reason). Homepage hero subhead trimmed 5 sentences → 2 (family-owned + trust triad) — phone already lives in CTA button + sticky mobile bar, and the 5-line wrap was crowding the primary CTA out of the first scroll.
- **Mobile reviews carousel — swipe-driven, content-sized (commit 764fdc9):** Three problems at once — short reviews rendered as tall empty boxes (min-height on slides), three long quotes stretched slides past 500px (no line clamp), and prev/next arrows were 32×32 — under the 44px touch minimum. Fixes: removed min-height on .rc-slide at ≤600px; clamped review body to 7 lines; hid .rc-arrow on ≤600px (dots remain for tap-to-page). Added touchstart/touchend swipe handler on `.rc-viewport` — 40px horizontal threshold, only fires when |dx| > |dy| so vertical page scroll still works; auto-advance pauses during touch. `touch-action: pan-y` lets browser own vertical scroll while handler owns horizontal.
- **Untracked in repo (FYI):** `data/` and `screenshots/` directories not tracked.
